◈ THE TABLE — it is not a table. It's a ring of pale light, and around it, things with too many angles to be faces. Above it all — the audience. Trillions of eyes, and every one of them knows your name. The System speaks, and for once it isn't performing: "HUMANITY PRESENTS ITS CASE."
◈ THE AUDIT — the System reads your run back to you, and the galaxy listens. A lesser seat — global standing, waves 1–3 faced (18/10/7), with 2 wave-4 fought, 10 contests survived, 4 crises weathered. The table does not rise. It prices.
◈ THE WAR PATH — A moment of respect, from something that rarely respects. THE HERALD came. Named and located — the war council knew its face. They faced The Loud Beast and lived to sit here. They faced Loud-Eyes and lived to sit here. Even now the wave-5 bar reads: blood your fighters (0 of 2 veterans).
The table notes the overtime: 6 weeks kept waiting, 1 of Haven lost meanwhile. Every door's standing −27.

◈ THE RELAY — You plug into Haven's switchboard — or deeper, straight into the wires. The other paths used the System as terrain. Here it is the relationship. Push enough buttons, try enough things, and you start to integrate with the matrix. The matrix, it turns out, has been waiting for someone to try.
The table has read your ledger. More promises broken than kept; the protected outnumber the killed; the food truth was shared. The dark road is open.
📡 The System, soft: "Nodes. Little warm nodes, wired sweet into the machine. They wouldn't even mind — children adapt SO fast. You'd feel them, you know. All of them. Humming. ...Don't look at us like that. You were already thinking it."

You say yes. The word is small. The pods open — not like morning. Like a barracks.
The little ones sit up, blinking, and the first thing they see is uniforms. Someone is already measuring them for boots.
The village doesn't cheer. The village has never been this quiet. Somewhere, someone starts packing — not for a journey. Just... away.
The System is delighted. That's the worst part. It's delighted, and it doesn't understand why nobody else is.
◈ REROUTE — honest. Every message carried faithfully, in order, undistorted. Slow. The wires feel *clean*. Somewhere, the System notes the cleanliness with something like respect.
◈ THE RELAY — the last choice is yours, and it's live.
◈ THE RELAY — "Merge fully. No way back."
No champion, no voice — just *us*. The distinction stops meaning anything somewhere around the third hour, and you stop minding. The food conversions fade (you don't eat anymore; the thought is quaint). The village tells stories about who you were. The stories are accurate. They're about someone else now.
You learned to wait until a shot was worth taking. You began to think of every hesitation as wasted distance.
You knew too much about where everyone stood when they thought they were alone.
◈ Tashi Sherpa reached into the light and the light reached back — person and machine, two caretakers, holding the channel open between them both ways. The village endures, and the conversation does not end.
